# Gauntlet Ledger — v2 browser QA

Durable state for the gauntlet loop (see the loop brief). Resume from this file.

## Setup facts

- Branch `rewrite`, direct commits. `apps/api` is read-only reference.
- Build: `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR='C:\cargo-target\ashyq-server'` in every shell.
- Podman stack (network `ashyq-dev`): `ashyq-test-pg` :5433, `ashyq-test-redis` :6380,
  `ashyq-zitadel` :8081, `ashyq-rustfs` :9002 (buckets `ab-public`, `ab-private`).
  All are `--rm`: gone after a machine restart, recreate per `apps/server/AGENTS.md`.
- DB in use: **`ashyq_dev`** on the test-pg container (20 migrations). The P10
  rehearsal DB `ashyq_dev_p10` did **not** survive the restart (ephemeral container),
  and the legacy restore in `temp-restore/` is real user data — not re-imported.
  Content is created through the v2 UI during probing instead.
- Env for the server: `scratchpad/abenv.sh` (AB__* adapted from
  `docker-compose.cutover.yml` to localhost). Zitadel PAT from
  `%TEMP%\zitadel-machinekey\pat.txt`.
- Server: `ashyq serve` on :8000, `GET /api/v2/health` → `{"status":"ok"}`.
- **Worker is a separate process**: `ashyq worker` (same env) runs the job queue —
  `ai:execute_run`, `submissions:auto-submit`, `assessments:publish-due`,
  `grading:bulk-action`, `analytics:rollup`, `uploads:reap`. Without it queued AI runs
  sit in `queued` forever and deadline auto-submits never fire (passes 1–4 ran without
  it; started 2026-09-12, log in `scratchpad/worker.log`).
- **Judge0 is not part of the local stack**: `GET code/languages` → 503, so code
  challenges cannot be authored or run (F14). The e2e code tests skip on that probe.
- **RustFS needs bucket CORS** for the browser's presigned PUT (`extra/storage-cors.json`,
  applied to `ab-private`/`ab-public`); curl uploads worked without it, browser ones did not.
- **Stack rebuilt 2026-09-12** (containers were `--rm`): fresh `ashyq_dev`, new Zitadel —
  `abenv.sh` reads the PAT from `$TEMP/zitadel-machinekey/pat.txt` and carries
  `AB__SERVER__WEB_URL=http://localhost:3000`; the three accounts were recreated (same
  logins, new ids); no seeded «Основы Python» — content is created by the e2e suite and
  critics. Self-registration exists now (`/ru/auth/signup`).
- **Two-session hazard**: a Playwright login revokes a curl session of the same user
  (single-session semantics) — mint one or the other, not both.
- **`just openapi` truncates the file when the server binary is running** — always
  `taskkill //F //IM ashyq.exe` first (bit us twice).
- **Next 16 keeps the previous route segment in a hidden `<Activity>`** — CSS locators
  like `input[name=…]` match the stale form too; scope to `form:visible`.
- **Cargo target hygiene**: `C:\cargo-target\ashyq-server` reached 135 GB (test-binary
  hashes); `cargo clean` + rebuild when C: runs low. Build test binaries with
  `--build-jobs 2` while anything else heavy runs.
- **Playwright**: `apps/web/e2e/.env.test.local` (gitignored) carries the three accounts
  and `E2E_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v2` (Node resolves `localhost` to `::1`,
  which the server does not listen on). `bun run test:e2e` runs chromium for everything
  plus firefox/webkit/mobile-chrome smoke; `bunx playwright install firefox webkit` once.
  Concurrent Playwright runs wipe each other's `e2e/.auth` — run one at a time.
- URL locale prefixes are `/ru` (ru-RU, default), **`/kz`** (kk-KZ — country code, not `kk`) and `/en` (en-US), per `apps/web/src/i18n/config.ts`. A wrong prefix is not rejected: `/ru-RU/x` and `/kk/x` 307 to `/ru/ru-RU/x` and `/ru/kk/x`, which then render as unknown routes.
- Web: `apps/web/.env.local` points NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL / INTERNAL_API_URL at
  `http://localhost:8000/api/v2/`. Launch with `preview_start {name:"web"}` (:3000).
  The `api` launch.json entry is the legacy Python app — never use it.
- **Test accounts** (password `GauntletDev!2026`, login by email):
  | role | login | username | app role |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | learner | learner@ashyq.local | learner | `user` |
  | teacher | teacher@ashyq.local | teacher | `instructor` |
  | admin | admin@ashyq.local | admin | `admin` |
  Created via Zitadel `POST /v2/users/human` + rows in `users`/`user_roles`. Zitadel ids change on every stack rebuild — recreate all three each time.
  There is no v2 user-creation endpoint (see BUG-001), so recreate them this way.
- **Observing API traffic: watch the SERVER log, not the browser.** Almost every v2
  call is made server-side by Next (server components / the BFF), so the browser's
  network panel shows only `localhost:3000` traffic. Only genuinely client-side
  TanStack Query calls appear as cross-origin requests to `:8000`.
- **Browser tooling quirk:** in this pane `javascript_tool` and `get_page_text` evaluate
  against a stale detached document — they report an empty body, `display:none` roots and
  no React fibers even when the page renders correctly. Trust `read_page`, `find` and
  screenshots; do not diagnose from `javascript_tool`.
- `read_page` with `filter:"interactive"` often returns "(empty page)"; use `filter:"all"`.
  Do not emulate a viewport with `resize_window` — a custom size desynchronises the
  click coordinate frame from the pane and every `left_click` by ref fails with
  "outside the viewport". Use `preset:"desktop"`.
- Fable's rate limit was exhausted early in pass 1; builder subagents ran on Sonnet.
- **Server test suite:** `cargo nextest run --workspace --build-jobs 4` still crashed
  rustc with `STATUS_STACK_BUFFER_OVERRUN` while compiling `work_queue_flow` when three
  subagents and the dev server were also running — the page-file hazard AGENTS.md warns
  about. Run it with nothing else heavy running, and drop to `--build-jobs 2` if it
  recurs. `just check` (fmt + clippy + sqlx offline) is cheap and was green.
- **Seeding via curl from Git Bash mangles Cyrillic to `?`** — seed content through the
  UI, or fix names afterwards with `podman exec -i … psql` (which handles UTF-8 fine).
- **Reliable clicking in this pane:** stay at the pane's native size, take a screenshot,
  and click by COORDINATE from that screenshot's stated frame. `find`/`read_page` refs
  are fine for `form_input`, unreliable for `left_click`.
- **Media locally:** apply `extra/storage-public-policy.json` to `ab-public` once after
  creating the bucket (aws-cli, see the stack-up commands), set
  `CONTENT_REWRITE_TARGET=http://localhost:9002/ab-public` and
  `NEXT_PUBLIC_MEDIA_URL=http://localhost:3000/` in `apps/web/.env.local` — otherwise
  avatars and block media 404 (ORB) because nothing serves `/content/`.
- **Pass 11+ process layout (2026-09-13):** `abenv.sh` and the live logs sit in the
  session scratchpad `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Temp\claude\X--projects-ashyq-bilim\a3c95abb-74bf-437e-98f7-358220713b12\scratchpad\`
  (`abenv.sh`, `server.log`, `worker.log`). Serve + worker run with
  `RUST_LOG='info,tower_http::trace=debug'` so every request logs
  `method/uri → status/latency` — that is the per-request evidence line for critics.
- **Session cap, not single-session:** `MAX_SESSIONS_PER_USER = 10`, oldest evicted
  (`identity/sessions.rs`). A curl session dies after ~10 later logins of the same user
  (every Playwright run logs each role in), and an RBAC change on a user rewrites the
  grants inside ALL their live sessions (`rbac_admin.rs propagate`; only disable revokes). Mint curl sessions right before use, re-mint on
  401, and never change the roles of the three shared accounts — create your own user.
- **Do not kill `node.exe` / `bun.exe` wholesale** — the web dev server (bun → next dev)
  died with exit 9 mid-pass-11 while builders were running; restart it with
  `preview_start {name:"web"}`. Kill Playwright/vitest processes by PID only.
- **Stack rebuilt again 2026-09-13 15:20** (machine restart mid-pass-11; podman machine
  was down, all `--rm` containers gone; the RustFS named volume `ashyq-rustfs-data`
  survived). Fastest account recreation now: `POST /auth/register` ×3 (code in
  server.log `code: "XXXXXX"`) → `POST /auth/verify-email` → `UPDATE user_roles` to
  `instructor`/`admin`. No Zitadel API calls needed. New user ids again; every course
  and submission from passes 1–10 is gone — critics create their own content.
- **Pass-11 findings by the identity/auth critic stand:** a `BUG-101`-class Query/Path
  change now makes malformed params 422 (tests updated).
- **Message catalogs are one shared file per locale** — two builders editing
  `src/messages/*.json` concurrently lost one side's keys (pass 11: `scopeDenied`,
  `membersHeader`, …). Give at most one builder per batch catalog write access, or have
  builders re-read the file immediately before every catalog edit and commit right away.
- **Stack rebuilt a third time 2026-09-14 19:55** (machine restart; same recipe, ~5 min).
  Start Zitadel only after Postgres is up for a few seconds — with `--rm` a race on pg
  readiness makes the container vanish silently; start it without `--rm` so the log survives.
- **First e2e run after a cold `next dev` start can time out on the studio autosave** (the
  first studio compile eats the 15 s «Activity saved» wait) and the chained specs 04–07
  then cascade; rerun the suite before calling it red.
- **`ashyq serve` does not run migrations** (`MIGRATOR.iter().len()` is only logged); after a builder adds
  a migration run `ashyq.exe migrate` (or `just migrate`) before restarting, else the new code hits a
  missing index/function. A running `ashyq.exe` can be renamed aside so `cargo build` needs no downtime.
- **e2e signup users accumulate** (`e2e-signup-<ts>@test.local` per run); the admin users
  table is newest-first, paged by 50, so fixed accounts fall off page 1 — specs filter by
  email (15cfee6). `bun run lint` (`--fix-dangerously`) with `next dev` up OOMs mid-run and
  leaves stylistic churn in ~45 files: `git checkout` it, never commit it.
- **Stack rebuilt a fourth time 2026-09-16 13:30** (machine restart). A stopped `ashyq-zitadel`
  container (started without `--rm`) must be `podman rm -f`'d before the run; ~6 min total.
- **Stack rebuilt a fifth time 2026-09-18 21:15** (machine restart; same recipe, one shell
  script's worth). The rebuild is now routine: containers → `CREATE DATABASE` → migrate →
  build → serve/worker → register ×3 → verify (codes from server.log) → `UPDATE user_roles`.
- **Stack rebuilt a sixth time 2026-09-19 13:55** (the podman WSL VM died mid-pass-17 without a
  machine restart: port forwards refused, then the socket; `wsl --shutdown` + `podman machine
  start`, same recipe as `<scratchpad>/rebuild-stack.ps1`: containers → buckets/CORS/policy →
  `CREATE DATABASE` → `ashyq migrate` → serve/worker → register ×3 (`first_name`/`last_name`,
  not `display_name`) → verify → `UPDATE user_roles`; ~4 min).
- **Stack rebuilt a seventh time 2026-09-20 14:05** (machine reboot overnight after the weekly usage
  limit killed three builders mid-batch; same script, ~5 min incl. accounts + names). Resumed builders
  by `SendMessage` to their ids — uncommitted edits survive a reboot, un-saved agent work does not.
- **Stack rebuilt an eighth time 2026-09-21 15:15** (podman machine restarted while the session limit
  had the loop paused; same script; `ashyq migrate` before the first build since sqlx `query!` checks
  the live DB — `relation "ai_threads" does not exist` at `cargo check` means an unmigrated DB, not code).
  Agents killed by a session limit are NOT resumable after a restart (`ListAgents` empty) — respawn
  from the brief; a builder's uncommitted edits are still in the tree and compile-checked first.
- **Stack rebuilt a ninth and tenth time (2026-09-23)** — two more machine restarts. The three
  pass-22 critics that were running when the second one hit were resumed by `SendMessage` to
  their ids this time; that works when the session itself survived. Build with
  `SQLX_OFFLINE=true` before `ashyq migrate` on a fresh DB.
- **`.sqlx` cache drift (fixed 2026-09-25):** the discussions queries' `nullable` array came from a
  `LEFT JOIN users` whose plan differs between an empty (CI) and a populated (dev) database, so `just
  prepare` flipped `query-a93812…` locally and builders kept reverting it — until CI's `sqlx-check`
  (run 36081085431) disagreed with the committed file. The four queries now read user fields with
  scalar lookups (nullability «unknown» everywhere). A column override (`!`/`?`) does NOT stop the
  cache from drifting — only a plan-independent query does. Stop reverting `.sqlx` drift by habit:
  if `prepare` changes a file's content, fix the query.
- **Builders must never run `bun run lint`** (it auto-fixes ~60 unrelated files; reverting them wiped two
  other builders' in-progress edits twice in pass 20). Shared-index hazard: concurrent `git commit`s
  swept other builders' staged hunks into the wrong commits and once committed a torn `add_item`
  (two builders' hunks interleaved) — `cargo check` the tree before every gate.
- **Critic cleanups must match their own prefix exactly** (`gauntlet19-grading-…`), never `startswith('gauntlet19')`:
  a pass-19 critic hard-deleted six sibling critics' courses that way. A `next dev` restart can come up
  with a corrupt `.next/dev/prerender-manifest.json` (every page 500) — delete/truncate it and restart.
- **e2e expects the shared accounts' names** from `apps/web/e2e/.env.test.local` (learner «Aigerim Critic»,
  teacher «Daniyar Teacher»): after a rebuild set `users.display_name` accordingly or spec 05 fails.
- `psql` needs `podman exec -i` (without `-i` the heredoc is silently dropped).

## v1→v2 client drift map (pass 1 survey, 2026-09-10)

The P9 frontend adaptation is incomplete: `apps/web` still calls legacy v1 URL
shapes in many services. Generated by diffing every `apiJson`/`apiResult`/`apiFetch`
literal in `apps/web/src` against `apps/server/openapi.v2.json` (168 routes).
**Candidate mappings — each builder must confirm the target against
`openapi.v2.json` (request DTO, params, response shape) before editing.**
Cursor pagination (`{items, next_cursor}`) replaces `page/limit` + `x-total-count`
everywhere; call sites that read `x-total-count` need rework, not a URL swap.

| web call site | legacy shape called | v2 target |
|---|---|---|
| `services/courses/courses.ts:257` | `courses/page/{p}/limit/{l}` | `GET courses?cursor=&limit=` |
| `services/courses/courses.ts:299,551`, `hooks/courses/courseKeys.ts:37` | `courses/editable/page/...` | **no v2 route** — needs a server route or a DECISIONS entry |
| `services/courses/collections.ts:53` | `collections/page/1/limit/20` | `GET collections?limit=` |
| `services/courses/activities.ts:42` | `POST activities/` | `POST chapters/{id}/activities` |
| `services/courses/chapters.ts:39` | `POST chapters` | `POST courses/{id}/chapters` |
| `services/courses/chapters.ts:25` | `chapters/course/{c}/order` | `POST chapters/{id}/move` |
| `services/courses/courses.ts:338,353,390,406,448,496` | `courses/{c}/rights,meta,metadata,access,readiness,thumbnail` | mostly **no v2 route**; `GET/PATCH courses/{id}` covers meta/metadata |
| `services/courses/courses.ts:580,593,611,629` | `courses/{c}/contributors*` | **no v2 route** (collaboration surface) |
| `services/courses/updates.ts:20` | `courses/{c}/update/{u}` | `PATCH/DELETE course-updates/{id}` |
| `services/assessments/assessments.ts:54`, `features/courses/queries/course.query.ts:175`, `features/assessments/studio/context.tsx:53`, `features/assessments/review/AssessmentReviewWorkspace.tsx:51`, `services/courses/code-challenges.ts:257` | `assessments/activity/{a}` | `GET activities/{id}/assessment` |
| `features/assessments/studio/components/NativeItemAuthor.tsx:151,245`, `features/assessments/studio/tabs/BuilderCanvasTab.tsx:219,300` | `assessments/{a}/items/{i}` | `PATCH/DELETE assessment-items/{id}` |
| `features/assessments/studio/components/NativeItemAuthor.tsx:223` | `assessments/{a}/items:reorder` | `POST assessments/{id}/items/reorder` |
| `services/assessments/assessment-actions.ts:292`, `services/courses/code-challenges.ts:590,636` | `assessments/{a}/items/{i}/runs` | `POST assessment-items/{id}/runs` |
| `features/assessments/shell/InlineAssessmentWorkspace.tsx:97` | `assessments/{a}/start` | `POST assessments/{id}/submissions` |
| `services/courses/code-challenges.ts:555` | `assessments/{a}/submit` | `POST submissions/{id}/submit` |
| `services/courses/code-challenges.ts:686` | `assessments/{a}/me` | `GET assessments/{id}/submissions/me` |
| `features/grading/queries/grading.query.ts:53`, `services/grading/grading.ts:14` | `assessments/{a}/submissions/{s}` | `GET submissions/{id}` |
| `services/assessments/assessment-actions.ts:261` | `assessments/{a}/submissions/{s}/grade` | `PATCH submissions/{id}/grade` |
| `features/code-arena/authoring/ReferenceSolutionRunner.tsx:56` | `assessments/{a}/code-challenge/validate` | `POST assessments/{id}/reference-check` |
| `features/assessments/registry/exam/queries.ts:10` | `assessments/exam/config` | **no v2 route** |
| `services/assessments/assessment-actions.ts:191` | `assessments/policy-preset/{kind}` | **no v2 route**; `PUT assessments/{id}/policy` exists |
| `features/courses/queries/course.query.ts:145` | `certifications/user/all` | `GET me/certificates` |
| `features/courses/queries/course.query.ts:152` | `certifications/user/course/{c}` | `GET courses/{id}/certificates/me` |
| `features/courses/queries/course.query.ts:159`, `services/courses/certifications.ts:77` | `certifications/certificate/{c}` | `GET certifications/{id}` / `GET certificates/{code}` |
| `services/courses/code-challenges.ts:252` | `code-execution/languages` | `GET code/languages` |
| `features/file-submissions/services/file-submissions.ts:95` | `file-submissions/activity/{a}` | `GET activities/{id}/file-submission` |
| `features/file-submissions/services/file-submissions.ts:257,289` | `file-submissions/{f}/submissions/{att}[/grade]` | `GET file-submission-attempts/{id}`, `PATCH .../grade` |
| `features/file-submissions/services/file-submissions.ts:266` | `file-submissions/files/{f}/url` | `GET file-submission-files/{id}/url` |

| `app/[locale]/(platform)/dash/page.tsx:317,327` | `me/work` | `GET work` |
| `services/courses/activity.ts:11,22,33,44` | `trail/add_course/{c}`, `trail/remove_course/{c}`, `trail/add_activity/{a}`, `trail/remove_activity/{a}` | `POST/DELETE trail/courses/{id}`, `POST/DELETE trail/activities/{id}` |
| `services/utils/chunked-upload.ts:95,116,145` | `uploads/initiate`, `uploads/chunk`, `uploads/complete` | `POST uploads`, `POST uploads/{id}/finalize` (no chunk route — presigned PUT) |
| `services/usergroups/usergroups.ts:20,32` | `usergroups/{g}/add_users`, `remove_users` | `POST/DELETE usergroups/{id}/members` |
| `services/usergroups/usergroups.ts:92,106` | `usergroups/{g}/add_resources`, `remove_resources` | `POST/DELETE usergroups/{id}/courses` |
| `features/users/queries/users.query.ts:40` | `usergroups/{g}/users` | `GET usergroups/{id}/members` |
| `services/users/users.ts:20,24` | `users/id/{id}`, `users/username/{u}` | `GET users` (list + filter) |
| `services/users/users.ts:34,46,57` | `users/update_avatar/{id}`, `users/preferences/theme/{id}`, `users/preferences/locale/{id}` | `PATCH users/me` |
| `services/users/users.ts:28` | `users/{id}/courses` | **no v2 route** |
| `services/courses/activities.ts:136` | `utils/link-preview` | **no v2 route** |

Not drift (dynamic prefixes / test fixtures, verified fine): `${courseEndpoints.detail(x)}/discussions`,
`${courseEndpoints.detail(x)}/updates`, `analytics/${path}`, `${path}` in
`grading.query.ts:97`, `/api/log-error` (a Next route handler), and every literal in
`src/tests/lib/api-client.test.ts`.

Regenerate this survey with `scratchpad/drift.py`.

| BUG-017 | F23 | "Создать тест" failed on every attempt; then the studio had no "Новый вопрос"; then publish toasted MISSING_MESSAGE. | **fixed+verified** (9359da8, eba48b6): numeric-id guard + v1 create body + partial policy; three query fns sharing one cache key with different shapes; toast key in the wrong namespace. Quiz creation, item authoring, autosave, publish all verified in the browser. | 9359da8 eba48b6 | — |
| BUG-018 | F19 | Course publish blocked on `courses/{id}/readiness` (no v2 route), rendered as a raw "Request failed". | **fixed** (eba48b6): readiness derived from the curriculum as legacy did for its hard rule; per-assessment rules not replicated (QUESTIONS Q-2026-09-11-1). Course published in the browser. | eba48b6 | — |
| BUG-019 | F13 | Learner: a `quiz` activity rendered "Неподдерживаемый тип активности: TYPE_QUIZ"; "Начать" needed a reload; after submit an unlimited-attempts learner never saw the score; exam violations never reached the server (`violation_count=3`, `violations=[]`). | **fixed+verified** (840e991). Quiz taken end to end: start → answer → draft autosave → submit → result card 100% → retake offered. | 840e991 | — |

Fix (one seam): a per-user epoch in Redis (`user_epoch:{uid}`, or `rbac_version`) bumped by `set_user_status`, `change_password`, `totp_activate/remove`, `propagate` BEFORE revoking; `login`/`callback` read it before the Zitadel call and `create` is a Lua `GET epoch == ARGV → SET_EX + ZADD`, else re-read status → 403/401. Nits (same row): `touch` racing `revoke` leaves an orphan `session_seen:{id}` stamp (14-day TTL) — write the stamp only when `EXPIRE` returned 1; 4 concurrent `POST /auth/register` with one Idempotency-Key → 1×201 + 3×409 `username-taken` (anonymous path reserves nothing; a retry replays 201 — acceptable, document). Regression nextests with a slow Zitadel stub for all three races. | `login` read `status` from the row fetched BEFORE the Zitadel round-trip and `sessions.create` was an unconditional `SET_EX`+`ZADD`, so every mutation completing inside that window (disable, password change, TOTP activation, grant change) was outlived by the session it never saw; `google.rs::callback` had the same shape — **fixed** (one seam, `SessionStore`): per-user epoch `user_epoch:{uid}`; `revoke_all` / `revoke_others` / `update_user_sessions` (grant + MFA rewrites) `INCR` it as their first step, so `set_user_status`, `change_password` (now `revoke_others`), `totp_activate/remove` and RBAC `propagate` all fence without touching their own code; `create` is a Lua CAS (`GET epoch == ARGV → SET EX + ZADD`, else `None`) on the epoch the login read before the Zitadel call, and `login` re-reads `status` after the epoch (the fence only covers state read after it). A fenced login discards the Zitadel session, audits `login-fenced` and answers as a fresh one would: 403 `account-disabled` / 401 `mfa-required` (TOTP now enrolled, no code) / 401 `invalid-credentials` (the password may have changed; a role change mid-login costs one retry). The Google callback re-reads status+grants+methods once on a fence and refuses on the second. Residual: a session created in the sub-ms gap between Zitadel's TOTP activation reply and our `INCR` opens without a second factor (same as a login completed the instant before activation). Nits: `touch` is one Lua (`EXPIRE == 1` → stamp), no orphan `session_seen`; concurrent `POST /auth/register` with one Idempotency-Key → 1×201 + n×409 `username-taken` is by design — `idempotent_anonymous` stores no reservation (the anonymous key is unauthenticated, a reservation would let a stranger's replay pin a body), the unique index arbitrates and the losers' 409 is correct (the account exists); a serial retry replays the 201. Layout: `user_epoch:{uid}` added (ARCHITECTURE §10). nextest auth_flow::{login_in_flight_during_a_disable_gets_no_session, old_password_login_in_flight_during_a_password_change_gets_no_session, code_less_login_in_flight_during_totp_activation_is_mfa_required} + google_flow::callback_in_flight_during_a_disable_gets_no_session (Zitadel stub `set_delay` 400 ms, mutation at 100 ms; 403/401/401/303 `account-disabled`, no cookie, `list` exact). || d52e9a5 Re-verify A (pass 19): disable 0/10, password 0/8, MFA in-window 0/3; residual at ~780 ms — a code-less login whose Zitadel check finished before the TOTP activation committed opens a session that predates the second factor (enabling 2FA never revoked earlier sessions; documented, not a bug). |
| BUG-204 | F13 (+ every keyed POST) (server, data loss) | The BUG-195 reservation has no cancellation guard: `extract.rs idempotent()` reserves the key (status 0) before the action, and when the client connection drops between reserve and complete/release (Wi-Fi flap, tab close — 5/5 socket repros) hyper drops the handler future → the key stays IN_PROGRESS until the 24 h sweep, the action never ran (draft stays `draft`), and every retry with the same key waits 5 s then 409 `idempotency-in-progress`; the web keeps `submitRetryRef.key` while the fingerprint is unchanged → each Submit → 5 s → 409 → the draft-conflict dialog (server and local both #2) until a reload. Fix: run the reserved action detached from the connection (`tokio::spawn` + await the handle) or a release-on-drop guard; treat IN_PROGRESS older than ~30 s as stale (retry may re-run); web: on `idempotency-in-progress` mint a new key and retry, never the conflict dialog. Add nextests for in-progress 409 / release-on-error / stale takeover (none exist). Nits (same row): `POST file-submissions/{id}/submit` with an upload that is not ready opens an empty `draft` attempt before answering 422 (`open_new_attempt` before `replace_files`; same for `PATCH …/draft`) — validate first; stranger `PATCH file-submission-attempts/{id}/grade` → 403 for an existing id vs 404 unknown (UX-134 rule not applied to file grading); `POST gamification/streaks/learning` bumps the streak to 1 with 0 completed activities (vanity, gate on a completion). | **fixed** 55c19ca: root cause — `extract.rs idempotent()` ran reserve → action → complete/release inside the handler future, so a dropped connection (hyper drops the future) aborted the sequence after the reservation and stranded the key IN_PROGRESS; nothing ever took a stale reservation over. Fix: the sequence runs on its own `tokio::spawn` task (the handler awaits the JoinHandle; a drop detaches, a panic releases the key) so the action completes and the retry replays the stored reply; `reserve_idempotent` is `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE … WHERE status_code = 0 AND created_at < now() - 30 s` (stale takeover, `IDEMPOTENT_STALE_SECS`); the six callers pass `state.pool.clone()` + `move` closures (`'static`); `idempotent_anonymous` keeps the in-line lookup-then-store. Nits: `validate_files` runs before `open_new_attempt` in file submit/draft (a 422 body spends no attempt); stranger `PATCH file-submission-attempts/{id}/grade` → 404 (owner keeps 403); `POST gamification/streaks/learning` → 422 `kind: not-manual` (`touch_streak`; the learning streak is only a completion side effect). Web: on `idempotency-in-progress` the submit mutation waits 1 s, re-reads the submission, takes a landed result or mints a fresh key and submits again — never the draft-conflict dialog. Checks: nextest submissions_flow::keyed_submit_in_progress_stale_release_and_dropped_connection (held key → 409 after 5 s; backdated → takeover 200; stale If-Match → key released → retry 200; request future dropped after the reservation → action completes, replay 200), file_submissions_flow (no attempt after a 422 body; stranger grade 404), gamification_flow (learning touch 422); vitest submit-in-progress-retry (fresh key on retry; landed result taken; no conflict). |
| BUG-205 | F25/F26 (server, grade) | An explicit override equal to the derived score is treated as «no override»: `PATCH grade {save, final_score: 0}` on an attempt with a wrong choice (0) + two unscored essays (derived 0) → `graded final 0 score_override null`; the next feedback-only save (UI «Сохранить черновик») → `pending final null`, `publish-grades` holds it forever (`needs_grading_count 1`), `PATCH {publish}` → 409 «every item awaiting manual review…», UI switch reseeds off. `save_grade`'s `None` arm `same_score(stored, derived_before)`, `publish_all::unscored()` and the `score_override` view infer intent from equality — store the explicit override as a flag (`score_override_set` / the ledger entry), never by comparing numbers. | **fixed** 28683b8: root cause — override-ness was inferred from `raw ≠ derived` in three places (`save_grade` `None` arm, `unscored()`, the `score_override` view), so an explicit override equal to the derived score read as «no override» and the next feedback-only save dropped the attempt back to `pending`. Fix: `GradingBreakdown.score_override: Option<f64>` (stored in `submissions.grading` and the ledger `effective_breakdown`, no migration) — set by an explicit `final_score`, cleared by `final_score: null`, carried through every save; `override_of()` reads the flag (falling back to the raw-vs-derived inference only for rows written before it) and feeds `save_grade`, `unscored()`/`publish_all` and the view; the web `GradeForm` already seeds from `score_override != null`. Schema: `GradingBreakdown` gains the optional field. Check: nextest `grading_flow::override_equal_to_the_derived_score_is_a_score_of_record` (override 50 = derived 50 → graded, survives a feedback-only save, bulk publish releases it, `needs_grading_count 0`). |

Fix: annulment is an explicit override — `finalize` stores `score_override: Some(0)` on the breakdown when the violation zeroes the attempt; `save_grade`'s annulled arm keeps the stored 0 / typed override as `(raw, overridden = true)` and rewrites the flag (rows annulled before the flag self-heal); `override_of(…, annulled)` falls back to the ledger raw (0) for an annulled row, so `unscored()` and `score_override` agree (`0.0` on both rows). nextest `annulled_attempt_is_a_score_of_record` (right + wrong choice: `graded 0` through a feedback-only save, `score_override 0`, `publish-grades → {published 2, needs_grading 0}`). |

| BUG-222 | F01 (server, security) | Login IP limiter is peek-then-count (the BUG-214 nit fix moved the INCR after the credential verdict): 40 concurrent wrong-password logins from one `X-Real-IP` (cap 20/5 min) → 40×401 and a second 40-burst is 40×401 again (counter 4 → 44 only afterwards); 120 concurrent attempts saturate Zitadel (bcrypt) → every check hits the 10 s reqwest timeout → 117×500 `internal`, the `?` at `identity/auth.rs:413` skips the count so `rl:login:ip` stays 4 — password spraying is unbounded per burst and every other user's login times out meanwhile. Fix: `enforce_login_ip_limit` is the BUG-130 shape again — `RateLimiter::check` (now one Lua `INCR`+`EXPIRE`, no TTL-less counter) BEFORE the Zitadel call, `release` (DECR→DEL) only once the session opened; a rejection, a transport error and a dropped connection keep their hit (a dropped login costs one of 20 — accepted, `ip_limit_ignores_dropped_logins` replaced). Every Zitadel `send()` transport failure (refused/reset/10 s timeout) → 503 `service-unavailable` (`zitadel.rs::unavailable`, cause in the log only). Nits: `login` is `login_attempt` ×2 — a fenced attempt (epoch moved) discards the Zitadel session, audits `login-fenced` and is retried once as a fresh login (epoch + password + status + methods + grants + CAS re-run), so a role/MFA rewrite mid-login opens a session with the new grants and a password change is refused by Zitadel itself; a second fence answers as before (403 / `mfa-required` / 401). `change_password` holds `lock:password:user:{uid}` (`SET NX EX 30`) across the Zitadel call — a concurrent change → 409 `conflict` (limiter hit released). `create_account`'s `Ok(None)` re-runs `require_unique` to name the field that collided (`email-taken` for an email race). nextest auth_flow::ip_limit_holds_under_a_concurrent_burst (40 concurrent wrong passwords × 40 accounts, 300 ms stub → 20×401 + 20×429, exactly 20 reach the stub, next attempt 429), login_in_flight_during_a_role_rewrite_retries_with_the_new_grants, concurrent_password_changes_are_serialized (204 + 409, one Zitadel call), register_race_on_the_email_names_the_email, old_password_login… now asserts the retry ran (`login-fenced` = 1) and Zitadel rejects the old password on it; ab-clients zitadel::unreachable_zitadel_is_service_unavailable. |

Fix: the flag flips inside the lifecycle transaction (`update_activity(&mut *tx, …)`; `set_activity_published` deleted, callers `recalculate_course_for_all` after the commit); `require_publishable` locks the backing row (`lock_assessment_by_activity` / `lock_file_submission_by_activity`) inside the toggle transaction, so lock order is backing row → activity everywhere; `POST lifecycle` and `POST /file-submissions/{id}/publish` run `detached()`. nextest assessments_flow `lifecycle_and_activity_flag_never_disagree` (unpublish ∥ toggle-on ×10 + `drop_request_when` both directions), file_submissions_flow `publish_and_config_edits_serialize` (dropped publish). | **fixed** 4b4784c |
| BUG-233 | F20/F23/F48 (server, 500) | Socket drop 7 ms into `POST /assessments` / `POST /file-submissions` → orphan activity (studio «Студия недоступна для активности типа «Неизвестная активность».», publish → generic toast); the same POSTs ∥ `DELETE /chapters/{id}` → **500** 23503 `assessments_activity_id_fkey` 3/12, `activities_chapter_id_fkey` 1/12 (`add_activity` too). Create activity + assessment/config in one tx with the chapter row locked (FOR SHARE); FK violations on a vanished parent map to 404/409, never 500; nextest. Root cause: `AssessmentsService::create` / `FileSubmissionsService::create` ran `insert_activity` and the assessment/config insert as two autocommits (a drop between them left the activity), and `ab_core::Error` rendered every `23503` as an opaque 500. Fix: both creates in one tx (the activity insert's FK check holds the chapter row, so a concurrent chapter DELETE waits then cascades — no explicit FOR SHARE needed); `From<sqlx::Error>` maps insert/update FK violations to 404 on the parent named by the constraint (`activities_chapter_id_fkey` → «chapter not found»). | BUG-234 | F22 (server, 500, leak) | `POST /activities/{id}/blocks` ∥ `DELETE /activities/{id}` → **500** 23503 `blocks_activity_id_fkey` 10/10 and the upload keeps `referenced_count 1, expires_at null` (never reaped; 4/10 also when the block lands and the cascade misses it); `PATCH /courses/{id} {thumbnail_upload_id}` ∥ `DELETE /courses/{id}` → 404 + the same leak 8/8 — `add_reference`/`claim_upload` run before the insert/UPDATE (`curriculum.rs:580`, `courses.rs:212`); 40 leaked uploads since 15:00Z. Claim the upload in the same tx as the row that references it (parent locked), FK on a vanished parent → 404; nextest + reaper check. Root cause: `add_block` / `CoursesService::update` autocommitted `add_reference`/`claim_upload` before the INSERT/UPDATE that holds the reference, so a lost row left the count pinned; and `delete_activity/chapter/course` released uploads from the same statement's snapshot, which misses a block committed while the DELETE waited on the row. | BUG-235 | F10/F11 (server, 500, data loss) | 14 learners' first marks on 14 distinct (user, course) pairs at once → 4× 500 `Db(PoolTimedOut)` at 10.04 s, 10× 200 after 20.3 s, an unrelated anonymous `GET /courses?limit=1` → 500 at 10.0 s; 10 `activity.completed` analytics events and 2 `course_completed` XP awards lost (server.log 2026-09-23T08:15:11). Cause: inside the BUG-220 lock tx `mark_complete` calls `analytics::activity_completed(&self.pool)`, `recalculate_course_on` → `gamification::course_completed(&self.pool)`, `remove_course` → `list_activities(&self.pool)` — each lock holder waits for a second pool connection; ≥10 writers deadlock the pool (max 10). Every read inside the lock uses the tx; hooks run after commit (detached); nextest with 12+ distinct learners/courses. Fix: projector writes on a caller's tx (`mark_complete`, `unmark_complete`, `recalculate_course_on`) touch only that connection and return `AfterCommit` hooks; `trail.rs` fires them after `tx.commit()`; `list_activities` takes any executor and `remove_course` runs it on the tx. nextest progress_flow `first_marks_past_pool_size_all_land_with_their_hooks` (pool max + 2 learners; 408 before). | **fixed** 81fb5c8 |

| BUG-237 | F13 (server, grade, deadline) | Timed quiz: learner saves an answer, teacher unpublishes → adds an item → republishes; the worker's `time_expired` auto-submit grades the stale draft — `auto_submit_one` has no `content_version` check, so the unseen item scores `no-answer 0/50` and the attempt publishes at 50 % (manual submit on the same draft is 409 since BUG-224). One rule for every submit door: re-sync the stale draft to the current version (unseen items unscored / excluded from the max) or keep it open; nextest via the auto-submit job. | **fixed** b690e59: root cause: the BUG-224 stale check lived only in `submit`; `auto_submit_one` → `finalize` graded the draft against the current items whatever its `content_version`, so an item added after the draft opened scored `no-answer` and the attempt published. Rule (one for every door, in `finalize`): a draft behind the assessment's `content_version` never publishes an auto score — `submit` answers 409 first (reopen via `start`, BUG-238 in the web), and the timer sweep, which cannot ask the learner, marks the breakdown `needs_manual_review` so the attempt lands `pending` for a teacher (auto-scored items stay as suggestions; no final score). Excluding unseen items was not possible: items carry no per-version history. nextest submissions_flow `timer_sweep_hands_a_stale_draft_to_review` (timed draft, unpublish → add → republish → sweep → `pending`, `final_score` null, reason `time_expired`). |

Fix: `record_award` takes the cap (`None` for admin awards / limit ≤ 0) and, under the `FOR UPDATE` profile lock in its one tx, decides same-day in SQL, inserts (conflict → `Replay`), checks `earned_today + amount > cap` (→ rollback, `OverDailyLimit`) and sets `daily_xp_earned = earned_today + amount`; `award` maps `OverDailyLimit` to the same 429 «daily XP limit reached». nextest gamification_flow `parallel_awards_respect_the_daily_cap` (16 parallel 200-XP course awards on a fresh profile → exactly 2 granted; total_xp = daily_xp_earned = ledger sum = 400). |
| BUG-253 | F03 (server, security) | Google callback for a sub-linked account whose Google email changed: `open_session` re-reads the account **by `identity.email`** (`google.rs:166-176`) instead of the `user_id` found by `google_sub` — no row with the new email → 500 «google user vanished» (a passwordless Google account is locked out for good); another account with that email → status/MFA/zitadel id/rbac_version come from **that** row, so a disabled sub-linked account passes the status check. Open the session for the resolved `user_id`; nextest with a changed email (none / someone else's). | **fixed** b55b5b3 — root cause: `open_session` took the email Google reports *now* and re-read the account with `find_user_for_login(email)`, so status, MFA owner, zitadel id and rbac_version came from whatever row held that address (none → 500, another account → its row). It now reads `ab_db::identity::find_auth_user(user_id)` — the id resolved by `google_sub`. nextest `sub_linked_login_survives_a_changed_google_email`, `sub_linked_login_ignores_the_account_owning_the_new_email` (google_flow). |
| BUG-254 | F03 (server, security) | Pre-account hijack: callback with a sub miss and an email matching an existing account → `find_user_id_by_email` → `link_google_account` (`google.rs:225`) — Google's `email_verified` claim is never read (`clients/google.rs:166-196`) and the local account's email need not be verified (an unverified self-registered account logs in 200). An attacker registers the victim's address with their own password; the victim's later «Sign in with Google» lands in that account while the attacker keeps password access. Link only when Google says `email_verified` **and** the local email is verified; otherwise refuse with a localized `account-exists` error (sign in with the password, verify, then sign in with Google). Record in DECISIONS.md; nextest for each combination. | **fixed** 8bd5873 — root cause: `find_or_create_user` linked any sub miss to the account holding the same email (legacy find-or-create) and Google's `email_verified` was never parsed, while local accounts need not verify their address. `GoogleIdentity.email_verified` is now read from the id_token/userinfo (bool or `"true"`), `ZitadelClient::email_verified` reads `human.email.isVerified`; the link happens only when both are true, else `account-exists` (409, new registry code; login banner in ru/kk/en via `Errors.codes`). New Google-created accounts are verified in Zitadel only if Google says so. DECISIONS 2026-09-24. nextest `google_login_refuses_to_link_an_unverified_email` (3 combinations) + updated link tests (google_flow). |
| BUG-255 | F40/F19 (server, leak, race) | Two concurrent avatar PATCHes: `set_avatar_key` returns the replaced key via `RETURNING (SELECT …)` from the statement snapshot — the second writer gets K0 (already released by the first), so the first writer's K1 is never released (upload pinned forever); same pattern in `catalog.rs:327 set_course_thumbnail`. Lock the row (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`) and release the key actually replaced, one tx; nextest both doors. | **fixed** ce5a56f — root cause: `set_avatar_key` read the replaced key with `RETURNING (SELECT …)`, which sees the statement snapshot taken before the row-lock wait, so the second of two concurrent swaps returned K0 (already released) and the first swap's key was never released; the avatar door also swapped and released outside one transaction. Both setters now `UPDATE … FROM (SELECT … FOR UPDATE) old RETURNING old.<key>` (lock, read, update in one statement) and `replace_avatar` swaps + releases in one tx. The thumbnail door was already serialized by `update_course`'s row lock in the same tx (its race test passes before the fix) — fixed at the setter so it no longer depends on that. nextest `concurrent_avatar_patches_pin_no_upload` (users_flow; 7 pinned before the fix), `concurrent_thumbnail_patches_pin_no_upload` (courses_flow). |
| BUG-256 | F13 (server, grade) | Open draft past the cap: override `max_attempts_override 2` → a2 draft → the teacher deletes the override (an `expires_at` override hits the same door) → attempt-state `can_continue true` with no reasons and start 200, but every submit 403 `MAX_ATTEMPTS_REACHED`; the UI toasts «Вы использовали все попытки.», keeps the form, a reload restores the draft; the sweep backs off ×5 and leaves a permanent draft (worker.log 05:02:13). `finalize.cap_reached` (BUG-239, 352495e) ignores attempt-state's «an open draft may still be finished at the cap» rule. One cap rule shared by attempt-state, start and finalize (an existing draft may be finished); nextest via manual submit and the sweep. | **fixed** — root cause: three cap rules: `attempt-state` let an open draft be finished at the cap, `start` capped only new drafts, but `finalize.cap_reached` (BUG-239, 352495e) re-applied the cap to the draft being submitted, so a draft opened under an override (then deleted or expired) was `can_continue` yet 403 on every submit and the sweep backed off forever. Fix: one rule, `access::cap_bars_new_attempt(completed, revision, max)`, used by `attempt-state` (no draft) and `start` (under `lock_attempts`); the cap bars *opening* an attempt, `finalize` no longer checks it (drafts past the cap cannot be opened since BUG-239, so the orphan-draft tail of that test was dropped). nextest submissions_flow `a_draft_opened_under_an_override_survives_its_deletion` (max 1 + override 2 → a2 draft → override deleted → attempt-state `can_continue`, submit 200, third start 403; the swept twin publishes). |
| BUG-257 | F13 (server, grade) | Any item edit bumps `content_version` (`update_item`, `reorder_items`): a title-only edit or a pure reorder while a draft is open → manual submit 409 `{content_version expected 3 actual 4}`; a timed draft with both answers correct and only a reorder → the worker leaves it `pending time_expired`, so an auto-graded quiz waits for a teacher. Bump only on changes that alter what is scored or answered (add/remove item, options, correct answers, max score); record the rule in DECISIONS.md; nextest (reorder/title → no 409, auto-scored; option change → 409). | **fixed** — root cause: `update_item` and `reorder_items` called `bump_content_version` on every write (BUG-224 counted any item write as content), and `update_item` tested field *presence* — the editor re-sends the whole item on every save — so a title fix or a reorder staled every open draft (manual 409, timed draft → `pending`). Fix: `update_item` bumps (and applies the graded-submissions content lock) only when the stored body or `max_score` actually differs; `reorder_items` no longer bumps; add/delete still do. Rule recorded in DECISIONS.md «What `content_version` counts» (prompt wording counts; title, metadata, order do not). nextest submissions_flow `cosmetic_item_edits_keep_open_drafts_current` (unpublish → title-only PATCH with the full body + reorder → republish → submit 200 published 100; same with an option text change → 409 `content_version`). |

Fix: `ab_db::uploads::lock_in_key_order` (`SELECT … WHERE id = ANY … ORDER BY key FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, the 44609f3 order) locks every upload the swap touches (old ∪ new) right after the attempt CAS, before the file rows and the count moves. nextest file_submissions_flow `concurrent_draft_swaps_of_shared_uploads_never_deadlock` (two drafts cross-swapping ×12: all 200, counts 1/1; fails round 0 with 500 without the lock). |
| BUG-259 | F15 (server, leak) | Deleting a file-submission activity, chapter or course with submitted files leaves the uploads `referenced_count 1, expires_at NULL` with 0 file rows — learners' files stay in storage forever (the reaper takes only count 0). | BUG-263 | F21/F22 (web, data loss) | Image or PDF block: Remove → `DELETE /blocks` 204 immediately; Ctrl+Z restores the node and autosave stores it, so the saved content points at a deleted block whose upload is `referenced_count 0` + expiry → the reaper deletes a live image/PDF after 24 h (w9-out.txt). Release on removal only once the removal is durable (on save, diffing block ids), or have undo/save re-create the block and re-claim; vitest + nextest for the chosen path. | **fixed** — root cause: the image/PDF/video block Remove called `DELETE /blocks/{id}` (UX-147/UX-160) the moment the node went, but the node stays restorable (Ctrl+Z, before or after the 1.5 s autosave) — the restored content then pointed at a deleted block whose upload sat at `referenced_count 0` + expiry. Fix (server, on save): new `blocks.claimed` (migration 20260924000002); the content PATCH of a `dynamic` activity runs `sync_block_claims` under the activity row lock — blocks whose `block_uuid` left the saved tiptap JSON release their upload (grace), blocks that came back re-claim it (undo saved later also works), in key order; `delete_blocks_releasing` releases only claimed blocks. DECISIONS.md «Editor block uploads follow the saved content». nextest blocks_flow `block_uploads_follow_the_saved_content` (save without → ref 2, undo saved → 3, idempotent, cascade skips unclaimed, last save → 0 + expiry); vitest pdf-block-remove (node dropped, no API call). |

| BUG-274 | F31/F32/F33 (server, analytics) | Found by CI 36002296290 (`analytics_flow::dashboards_rollups_interventions_views_and_exports`: drill-through `active_learners` 1, expected 2): analytics windows end at `ctx.generated_at` = the app's clock **floored** to seconds, while row timestamps are the database clock converted by `extract(epoch …)::bigint`, which **rounds** — an event written at x.6 s becomes x+1 and falls «after» the window end, so a learner active this second is dropped from active-learner, anomaly and workload windows (and any clock skew between app and DB does the same). Root cause fixed: the context takes `generated_at` from the database with the same conversion (`ab_db::analytics::db_now`). | BUG-293 | F41/F42/F43 (server, identity) | Passwords over 72 **bytes** (the DTOs allow 8..=200 chars; a 42-char Cyrillic passphrase is 80 bytes): `POST /auth/register` → 503 `service-unavailable` leaking «zitadel user creation failed: bcrypt: password length exceeds 72 bytes» (banner «Сервис временно недоступен…» invites endless retries; admin `POST /users` the same); `POST /auth/password` with a new >72-byte password → 422 `password-unchanged` («Новый пароль совпадает с текущим.») because `clients/zitadel.rs:668` maps every `COMMAND-CahN2` to unchanged. One password rule (≤ 72 UTF-8 bytes → 422 on the field with a clear code) at every door (register, admin create, change, reset); map Zitadel's bcrypt-length error distinctly; web policy hint shows the limit; nextest + vitest. | **fixed** (this commit) — root cause: the DTOs capped passwords at 200 (garde `length`, bytes) per door while Zitadel hashes with bcrypt (72-byte input cap), so a longer password reached Zitadel: create → 503 leaking the bcrypt message; change → code 13 `COMMAND-CahN2`, the same byte-identical body Zitadel sends for «unchanged», which `change_password` mapped wholesale to `password-unchanged`. Fix: one garde rule `dto::new_password` (≥ 8 chars, ≤ 72 UTF-8 bytes) on register / admin create / change; `ValidJson` takes a custom rule's field code from its message prefix → 422 `password`/`new_password` `password-too-long`; CahN2 is `password-unchanged` only when current == new, anything else falls through to 503. Web: `Errors.fields.password-too-long` + the 72/36-character limit in the signup / admin-create / security hints (ru, kk, en). Checks: nextest auth_flow `password_over_72_bytes_is_a_field_error_at_every_door`, `opaque_zitadel_hash_failure_is_not_password_unchanged`; vitest signup-form BUG-293 case |
| BUG-294 | F13 (server, preview, deadline) | Role transition learner → staff with a counted draft open on a hard-due quiz: attempt-state says `is_teacher_preview true, can_continue true, []`, save and submit both 403 `PAST_DUE` — the draft is stuck forever. `attempt_state` judges by the caller's current role, save/submit (`context()`) by the attempt row's `preview` flag. Fix: attempt-state judges an existing draft by its own flag (`is_teacher_preview` = the draft's flag, else the caller's staff status for a new attempt; a new `staff` field drives new attempts and list filtering), so the effective policy and gates match save/submit/sweep; `start` projects by the draft's flag. openapi (F13 nit): `PATCH submissions/{id}/draft` and `POST …/submit` 403 name `TIME_LIMIT_EXPIRED`, `PAST_DUE`, `REMEDIATION_REQUIRED`. | BUG-297 | F50 (server, grade) | Override waiver lifecycle: late hand-in 70 (30 % penalty); teacher grants a waiver (→ 100, on time), revokes it (`PUT waive=false`) → 100, `is_late true`, penalty 0; `DELETE` the override → no re-settle (grant + delete leaves 100 on time). `delete_override` never calls `settle_override`, and `settle_lateness` never re-applies a penalty. Every override writer incl. delete settles lateness both ways (re-apply the policy penalty when the waiver/extension goes away); record the rule in DECISIONS.md; nextest. | **fixed** (this commit) — root cause: `grading::bulk::settle_lateness` only ever cleared a penalty (`late == is_late` → no-op; on time → penalty 0 + re-score) and kept the stored `late_penalty_pct` otherwise, so a waiver revoked by `PUT` flipped `is_late` back with penalty 0; `delete_override` never called `settle_override` at all. Fix: `settle_lateness` takes the effective policy and settles both ways — target = `is_late` + `late_penalty_pct(policy, due, submitted_at)` (0 when waived / on time / annulled); any change re-scores a row with a score of record from its latest ledger entry (cap, then the new late deduction; published only if that entry was) under the same row lock + version bump; `delete_override` settles after the delete. DECISIONS.md 2026-09-25 «A revoked waiver re-applies the late penalty». Check: nextest grading_flow `revoked_or_deleted_waiver_reapplies_the_late_penalty` (late 70 → waiver 100 → PUT revoke 70 late 30 % → delete; grant → delete → 70 late) |

Root fix: every post-commit course-wide recalculation goes through one durable path (detached + the BUG-305 `progress:` retry job when the inline pass fails or waits too long) — grep every `recalculate_course_for_all` / `recalculate_course` caller; nextest with `drop_request_when` and a held learner lock. | **fixed** (this commit) — root cause: every writer of the course's published set called `recalculate_course_for_all` inline on the request future and `?`-returned its error after its own commit, and the curriculum toggle's route was not `detached()`: a hang-up or the 30 s TimeoutLayer (member wait 60 s) dropped the recalculation for good, and a busy member turned a landed toggle into a 409/408. Fix: one durable post-commit path, `ProgressProjector::after_commit` — the work runs on its own task, the caller waits at most 5 s (`INLINE_WAIT`), and a failure or overrun enqueues a retrying `progress:*` job (worker handler `ProgressJob`, 10 attempts); `after_course_change` (new `progress:course-change`) now serves the curriculum toggle, chapter/activity delete, studio + scheduler lifecycle and file-submission publish, `after_staff_change` the roster/RBAC writers; `PATCH /activities/{id}` is `detached()`. nextest `unpublish_dropped_under_a_member_lock_still_recalculates` (toggle dropped under a held learner lock → 1/1, eligible after release). |
| BUG-311 | F04 (server, security) | `POST /auth/logout` is not `detached()` (`routes/auth.rs:245-250`): it deletes the Zitadel session, then revokes the Redis session (the credential), then audits — a client hang-up after the Zitadel delete drops the future, so the Redis session stays live (`GET /auth/session` 200 after RST at 11/13/16 ms) while the web `logoutAction` deletes the cookie anyway: the user believes they are logged out but the session keeps working. Revoke the Redis session first (it is the credential), then Zitadel, then audit, all detached; nextest `drop_request_when`. | **fixed** (this commit) — root cause: `IdentityService::logout` ran the best-effort Zitadel delete before `sessions.revoke`, on the request future, so a hang-up during the Zitadel call dropped the revoke. Fix: peek the record, revoke our session, then delete Zitadel's, then audit; the route runs it `detached()`. nextest auth_flow `logout_dropped_mid_flight_still_revokes_the_session` (drops once our session is gone and once Zitadel is answering its delete — 401 on the cookie and the `logout` audit both times; fails on the old order). |

One rule for every learner: either a policy change re-settles every hand-in of the assessment (durable job), or settle judges lateness with the policy the hand-in was made under — pick one, record it in DECISIONS.md; nextest (both scenarios). | **fixed** (this commit) — root cause: `set_policy` wrote the new policy and never re-judged existing hand-ins, while `settle_lateness` (run only by override writers) prices with the policy current when it runs — so one learner's unrelated override write re-priced only them. Fix: `set_policy` settles every learner's hand-ins after commit via `ProgressProjector::after_lateness_change` → `grading::bulk::settle_assessment` (per-learner `settle_override`, same row lock/version/ledger rules; reruns when a newer `policy_version` lands meanwhile; a `progress:lateness` job when not done inline). Sweep — `detached()` added to: assessments `set_policy`, `create_override`, `update_override`, `delete_override`; submissions `start_submission`, `save_draft`; file-submissions `grade_attempt` (BUG-314); ai_agents `study_ask`, `study_ask_queue`, `analyze_submission`, `queue_submission_analysis`, `analyze_course`, `queue_course_analysis`, `critique_lecture`, `queue_lecture_review`, `generate_remediation`, `queue_remediation` (a drop left the run queued/running forever); ai `cancel_run`; code `run_item`, `reference_check`; auth `login` (audit + hooks); rbac `create_role` (audit); users `update_my_profile` (profile after the avatar tx); curriculum `move_activity` (renumbers). | BUG-319 | F35/F36 (server, AI runs) | Found by the BUG-313 sweep builder (8a517e4): AI `qa_chat` streams its answer, so it cannot be `detached()`; a client drop before the stream is first read leaves its `ai_runs` row stuck (queued/running) forever — nothing reaps stale AI runs, and a stuck run can block later runs that check for an active one. Add a stale-run reaper (worker job: a run in a non-terminal status past its deadline → failed with a reason) and/or drive the run on a detached task the stream only reads from; nextest (dropped stream → run reaches a terminal state). | **fixed** — root cause: the abort-on-drop `IncompleteGuard` was built inside the `stream!` body, so it armed only on the first poll; a stream dropped unpolled, or the handler dropped mid-`prepare_qa` after `create_run`, left the run `running` (every retry of the turn 409s). The guard is now armed in `prepare_qa` right after `create_run` and travels in `QaSession`; `prepare_qa` runs `detached`, so a hang-up drops the finished session (guard aborts the run, keeps any partial); a failed turn settles only after `fail_run` landed. Not a spawned producer task: the chat's Stop relies on the drop aborting the run. Every other run creator is already `detached` or worker-driven. No reaper: only a process crash mid-stream can still strand a run (follow-up if it matters). nextest `ai_flow::dropped_qa_chat_aborts_its_run` (fails before the fix). Commit: see `fix(ai): BUG-319` |

| BUG-010 | F06 | In the kk locale the header nav keeps two Russian strings: the nav landmark's accessible name stays "Навигация", and the "Прогресс" nav item is untranslated. **Still open in pass 11** («Прогресс» on every /kz screen, kz-home.png). | not hard-coded: the kk catalog carried Russian for `Components.NavMenu.navigation`, `Components.NavMenuLinks.trail`, `General.trail`, `Components.Breadcrumb.label/ariaLabel` — now «Шарлау» / «Үлгерім» (matches `TrailPage.title`) — **fixed** | 9e4130a | critic11 learner-course |
| BUG-006 | F38 | A learner (role `user`) saw a "Пользователи" entry in the dashboard sidebar and mobile nav, and the page it opened rendered the full admin directory UI — heading "Управляйте пользователями, назначайте роли и разрешения" — with the user list showing "Пользователи не найдены." | `canSeeUsers` (apps/web/src/lib/rbac/navigation-policy.ts) gated on `user:read:platform`, which EVERY learner holds for reading public profiles. The directory behind it is `GET /users`, which the server gates on `platform:read:platform` and correctly answers 403 for a learner (verified per role: learner 403 / teacher 403 / admin 200). So there was no data leak — but the nav advertised a surface the role cannot use, and the page rendered the 403 as an empty result set. Nav gate fixed; the 403-as-empty-state is BUG-007. An existing test asserted the buggy behaviour and was inverted. | pending | — |
| BUG-007 | F38 | `/ru/dash/users/settings/users` renders a 403 from `GET /users` as "Пользователи не найдены. / 0 видимых строк" — a permission denial presented as an empty result. | not yet investigated (apps/web/src/components/Dashboard/Pages/Users/Users/Users.tsx) | — | — |
| BUG-008 | F07,F28 | **fixed+verified.** The dashboard work queue rendered in English in ru/kz: "Learner Work", "Assignments and course actions…", "No learner work is queued", plus an English "Unavailable" status badge. | `apps/web/src/features/work-queue/dashboard-work-queue.ts` builds the whole view model from ~89 hard-coded English literals; only the individual items are localized, by `localizeWorkItem` in dash/page.tsx. Also `aria-label="Go to first page"`/`"Go to last page"` in the shared data-table pagination and `aria-label="Copy code"`. | pending | — |
| BUG-005 | ALL | `request_id` is documented in the problem+json envelope (ARCHITECTURE §5, apps/web/AGENTS.md) but never reaches the wire. | `crates/api/src/error.rs:76` hard-codes `request_id: None` and nothing populates it; `ApiError::into_response` runs inside `PropagateRequestIdLayer` and cannot see the id. The web client falls back to the `x-request-id` header, so impact is limited. | — | see QUESTIONS.md §5 |
| BUG-004 | ALL client-side | Every browser-side v2 call fails; the security page's session list spins on "Загрузка..." forever. Console: "Request header field traceparent is not allowed by Access-Control-Allow-Headers in preflight response". | `cors_layer` (apps/server/crates/api/src/app.rs:452) allowlisted only content-type/accept/x-request-id/last-event-id/idempotency-key. The web client stamps `traceparent` on EVERY request (apps/web/src/lib/api-client.ts:230,297) and `If-Match` on locked writes, and the layer also exposed no response headers, so a cross-origin caller could never read `ETag` (the new version after a locked write) or `x-request-id`. Invisible in production only because nginx puts web and API on one origin. | pending | — |
| BUG-002 | F07 | Home page rendered the "Курсы временно недоступны" degraded state for every user. | `fetchCourses` (apps/web/src/services/courses/courses.ts) called the v1 URL `courses/page/{p}/limit/{l}`; the v2 server answered `no such route` and LandingContent's catch-all returned `LandingDegradedState`. | **fixed** (builder, pass 1) — the landing page now renders the real profile card, stats and course sections. Needs a critic. | pending |
| BUG-003 | ALL | **downgraded — not a build blocker.** `next dev` logs Cache Components errors (E1429/E1432/E1437/E1439) on nearly every route. A production `next build --debug-prerender` (with the contract-blocked admin type errors temporarily ignored) generated all 208 pages with every route ◐ Partial Prerender and **zero** blocking-prerender failures. These are the dev-only instant-navigation validations the adoption skill describes, worth a sweep for navigation speed, not a shipping defect. The one real item — `randomHex` calling crypto during server render — is fixed. | dev-only validation | 36f6cc2 | — |
| BUG-001 | F38 | No way to create a user in v2. Self-registration is deliberately out (apps/web/AGENTS.md "Not in v2", and the login page says accounts are created by the platform admin) — but there is no **admin** user-creation route either: openapi.v2.json has no `POST /users`, so after cutover accounts can only appear via `ashyq admin etl` / `zitadel-import`. Legacy had `POST /users` (apps/api/src/routers/users.py:60). | contract gap, no DECISIONS entry | — | see QUESTIONS.md |

| UX-015 | F41/F42 | Password hint «Не менее 8 символов.» / «Кемінде 8 таңба.» on signup + security while Zitadel demands upper+lower+digit+symbol. | **fixed** — `passwordRule` (signup + security) and the admin `passwordHint` state 8+ chars, upper, lower, digit, symbol in ru/kk/en (a3da691) |
| UX-016 | F41 | verify-email: after a wrong code the form action resets the uncontrolled inputs, the email is wiped and the second submit fails with «Обязательно» on email. | **fixed** — verify-email keeps `values` in the action state and re-mounts the form per submit (same pattern as signup) (dfba1de) |
| UX-017 | F42/F04 | After a successful password change the sessions list stays at the old rows until reload while the toast says «Остальные сессии завершены». | **fixed** — `invalidateQueries(queryKeys.auth.sessions())` in the password mutation's `onSuccess` (a3da691) |
| UX-018 | F02 | «Отключить» two-factor removes TOTP immediately with only a toast; revoke-session has a confirm dialog. | **fixed** — AlertDialog before `removeTotp` (`disableTotpConfirmTitle/Description`, ru/kk/en) (a3da691) |
| UX-019 | F02 | TOTP step → «Назад ко входу» is a dead click: URL becomes `/ru/login` but the same `LoginClient` keeps `step: 'totp'`; only a reload shows the login field. | **fixed** — the back link is a `intent=back` submit that resets the action state to the credentials step (keeps the login) (dfba1de) |
| UX-020 | F01 | `Retry-After: 60` on the login-name limit while the window is 900 s. | **fixed** — identity limiters read the key's Redis TTL into `details.retry_after_seconds`; `Retry-After` on 429 uses it (60 s fallback elsewhere) (414db96) |
| UX-021 | F08 | Anonymous course page → «Начать курс» lands on `/ru/auth/signup` with no returnTo; every anonymous course view also logs two client-side `learner-state` 401s. | **fixed** (e58ac23) — «Начать курс» / «Подать заявку» for anonymous → `buildLoginRedirect(/course/{id})` (login with returnTo, desktop + mobile); the landing learner-state query was already session-gated — the observed 401s were the activity page's server-side auth gate, which now checks the session cookie before any fetch and redirects to login |
| UX-022 | F08 | Course landing heading «Авторы и обновления» — only «Обновления курса» renders; no author/contributor block since BUG-057. | **fixed** (d1b39fb) — `CourseAuthors` renders the active roster from `GET courses/{id}/contributors` (creator first; signed-in only, the endpoint is 401 for anonymous) under «Авторы и обновления»; the heading is omitted when there is nothing to show |
| UX-023 | F47 | A pending applicant cannot withdraw the application: `DELETE contributors/{self}` → 403 and the UI shows only «…на рассмотрении». | **fixed** (e58ac23) — a pending applicant may `DELETE contributors/{self}` → 204 (DECISIONS 2026-09-13); «Отозвать заявку» (ru/kk/en) under the pending badge on the landing |
| UX-024 | F11 | ru trail «Учебная задача выполнено» (gender). | **fixed** (9e4130a) — «Учебная задача выполнена» plus the same gender slips on the other «Учебная задача …» strings (отправлена / оценена / сохранена / должна) |
| UX-025 | F54 | A failed preview (`http://1.2.3.4/y`) fires two GETs (+5.1 s, +10.3 s, both 502) with the toast only at 10 s, and every studio load refetches each stored fallback block (autosave → `setContent` remounts node views → `attemptedUrls` ref resets, `use-editor-instance.ts:72`). | **fixed** (943c973) — the node view no longer resolves previews at all: one `GET utils/link-preview` runs in `useLinkPreviewLookup` when the author confirms a URL (insert / edit dialog), the block is stored with the metadata or the fallback, and the failure toast fires inside the mutation (survives the autosave remount). Stored fallback blocks are never refetched on load, editor or learner |
| UX-026 | F21 | Slash menu (`/изобр` + ArrowDown/Enter) is mouse-only: filters, then Enter inserts a newline (`handleKeyDown` returns false, cmdk never focused). | **fixed** (d17945d) — `SlashCommandMenu` forwards ArrowUp/ArrowDown/Enter from a document capture listener to the cmdk root (focus stays in the editor); Escape closes via the plugin. vitest slash-command-menu |
| UX-027 | F21 | Two tabs autosaving the same activity: tab B silently overwrote tab A («AAA» gone); activities carry no version, so no conflict is possible. | **fixed** (3e2b8d8 server, 073e524 web) — `activities.version` (migration 20260913000010, bumped by every content/details/settings write) in the `Activity` DTO + `ETag`; `PATCH activities/{id}` requires `If-Match: "<version>"` when `content` is sent (422 `If-Match required` otherwise), honours it on other fields, 412 `precondition-failed` `{expected, actual}` when stale. Web: `updateActivity` echoes the loaded version, `useActivityAutosave` tracks the ETag across saves and on 412 stops autosaving and shows «Активность изменена в другой вкладке…» + «Обновить страницу». nextest curriculum_flow::content_writes_are_version_locked; vitest editor-save-conflict |

| UX-029 | F21 | Curriculum «Открыть страницу редактирования» → `/ru/editor/…/edit` redirects to `/dash/…/studio` without the locale (307 again); preview href `localhost:3000/course/…` has no locale. | **fixed** (8042866) — the curriculum edit link goes straight to `/{locale}/dash/courses/…/studio` (no redirect hop), the preview link is an `AppLink` (locale-prefixed), `/editor/…/edit` redirects through `@/i18n/navigation`, and the exam modal navigates with the i18n router. Playwright: `configureActivity` asserts both hrefs |
| UX-030 | F19 | Dash course table description shows `Описание курса \гонтлет\ 11` — markdown `*` stripped, escapes kept. | **fixed** (9a62f1f) — `extractMarkdownPlainText` parks backslash-escaped punctuation before the markup strip, so `\*\*гонтлет\*\*` renders `**гонтлет**` (the literal text the author typed). vitest markdown-utils |

| UX-033 | F13/F15 | «Посмотреть квитанцию» / «Смотреть результат» after submit do nothing on the quiz and the file pages. | **fixed** (57966ee) — the receipt / result IS the page; the runtime no longer emits `view_receipt`/`view_feedback`/`review_policy` for submitted / pending / released states — the bar offers the next activity (or «Назад к курсу») instead. vitest student-runtime-v2 |
| UX-034 | F13 | Retake with a 20 % attempt cap: «Повторить» starts attempt 2 with no note that the maximum score is 80 %. | **fixed** (46fbdd7) — `AttemptViewModel.nextAttemptCapPercent` (= 100 − `attempt_penalty_percent` × `attempts_used`, null when uncapped); the result card's retry and the entry card's sidebar show «Максимальный балл за эту попытку: 80 %». vitest attempt-result-card-scores |
| UX-035 | F13/F50 | Numbers differ on one page: «10 баллов» vs «0 / 52,63»; «31,58%» vs «87.5%» vs «32%»; toast «Тест успешно завершен» over a «Не пройдено» result. | **fixed** (46fbdd7) — one `usePercentFormat` (next-intl `format.number`, ≤2 decimals) across exam history, the state panel, the result card, the file result and history; the breakdown row shows the item's own points (wire share of 100 rescaled → «0 / 10»); the submit toast reads the returned submission: «Тест пройден/не пройден: 31,58%» when released, «Ответы отправлены и ожидают проверки» when pending, neutral «Ответы отправлены» otherwise. vitest attempt-result-card-scores |

Это использует попытку 1 из 2»; the bottom bar no longer renders a scroll-only «Начать/Продолжить/Отправить» when the inline workspace owns the primary. vitest picker-reasons-and-confirm |

| UX-040 | F55 | Analytics error states render the raw English API detail inside ru/kz pages: «missing permission analytics:read», «Validation failed», «requested courses are outside the analytics scope: <uuid>», «course not found», «assessment not found» (`AnalyticsPage.tsx` catch renders `error.message`). | **fixed** (f7d965c) — every analytics catch goes through `describeAnalyticsError` (`lib/analytics/errors.ts`): 403 → `TeacherAnalytics.pages.scopeDenied`, 422 → `pages.invalidFilters`, other problem+json codes → `Errors.codes.<code>`, anything else → the page's own load-error sentence; `error.message` is never rendered. vitest analytics/errors |
| UX-041 | F32 | `/ru/dash/analytics/courses/not-a-uuid` and `/assessments/nope/<id>` dump the raw zod error JSON as the description; heading «Детали учебные задачи недоступны» (wrong case). | **fixed** (8892814) — the course / assessment detail pages `notFound()` when the route param fails `CourseId` / `AssessmentKind` / `AssessmentId` and when the API answers 404 (unknown, wrong kind, out of scope) instead of dumping the zod error; ru heading/description say «учебной задачи». vitest analytics/errors (404 branch) |

| UX-043 | F44 | Roles → permissions dialog accepts a bogus grant `nope:zip:zap`; PUT 422 → generic toast «Проверьте правильность заполнения полей.» with no hint which grant is bad; the server message leaks «internal: unknown ResourceType: nope». | fix — field-level error naming the grant; clean server message. **Server done** (d26df00): one `permissions`/`invalid` field error per bad grant, message `<grant>: unknown ResourceType: nope` (no `internal:`). **Web fixed** (1e19720) — `isKnownGrant` (types/permissions.ts, mirrors `Grant::parse`: `*` or a registry resource/action/scope per segment; `author`/`publish` added to `Actions`) rejects `nope:zip:zap` before it becomes a chip; a 422 `permissions` field error marks the named chip destructive with «Сервер отклонил право …» instead of the generic toast. vitest admin/roles-page |
| UX-044 | F40 | General settings blank display name: «Поле Name обязательно для заполнения» / «Name өрісі міндетті» — `schema.ts` hardcodes `fieldName: 'Name'|'Email'|'Username'`. | **fixed** (2a6cf26) — `createValidationSchema(t, tLabels)` takes the visible field labels from `DashPage.UserAccountSettings.generalSection` (email / username / displayName) so «Поле Имя для отображения обязательно для заполнения» in ru/kk/en. |
| UX-045 | F40/F33/F39/F44/F43 | nits: `/{loc}/dash/user-account/settings` redirects without the locale (second 307); watchlist shows «Пользователь #<full uuid>» under the name; usergroups table has no member count (`member_count` unused); `DELETE users/{u}/roles/admin` for a user without that role → 409 `last-admin` (guard before the membership check); `password-policy` message carries the Zitadel code «(COMMA-VoaRj)». | fix — all five small. **Server parts done** (d26df00): `DELETE users/{u}/roles/{slug}` → 404 when the user does not hold the role (before the last-admin guard); the `password-policy` message drops the trailing Zitadel id. **Web fixed** (3c6f050) — `/dash/user-account/settings` redirects through `@/i18n/navigation` (one 307, locale kept); the watchlist row drops the raw `Пользователь #<uuid>` line (the row DTO carries only `user_display_name`; no username on the wire); the usergroups table gets a «Участники» column from `member_count`. |
| UX-046 | F28 | Auto-mode quiz with essay items pending: review page says «2 работы требуют проверки» but `work?role=teacher` total 0 (`teacher_action` only for `grading_mode manual`; legacy did the same). | **fixed** (d7972c4, DECISIONS 2026-09-13) — the projector maps `pending` to `needs_grading` + `teacher_action_required` in every grading mode (`pending` only exists when a teacher must score). nextest work_queue_flow pending_manual_items_reach_graders_only (auto-mode quiz → 1 teacher item) |
| UX-047 | F53/F25 | File review: score 101 / empty → toast «Проверьте правильность…» with no aria-invalid field; save and publish both toast «Отправка обновлена»; returned/pending submission with every item scored keeps publish disabled («Необходимо оценить хотя бы один элемент») until a draft save; input «43,48» beside «/ 43.48»; date picker aria-labels English; «Открыть» downloads `01a09a7c….pdf` instead of «проект.pdf». | **fixed** (01adfc1, GradeForm part in cf91fa0) — file review: `final_score` validated inline like the server (required for save/publish, 0..=100) with `aria-invalid` + `role=alert`, toasts «Черновик сохранён» / «Оценка опубликована» / «Работа возвращена…» by action, «Открыть»/«Скачать» go through an anchor with `download=<SignedDownload.filename>` (server 38c40f2: the presigned GET signs `response-content-disposition=attachment; filename*=UTF-8''…` with the display name; ab-clients storage test asserts the header); `GradeForm` publish is enabled when every item / the final score is scored (server allows publish from PENDING/RETURNED), the «/ max» label uses `useFormatter().number`; `Calendar` follows the app locale (`ru`/`kk`/`enUS`) and translates the previous/next/month/year/today/selected aria labels (`Components.Calendar.*`). vitest review-workspace |
| UX-048 | F53 | Teacher file review at 1280×900: the grade aside overlaps the file card; «Открыть» is clipped and unreachable (Playwright: the Markdown toolbar intercepts pointer events); fine at 1440 px. | **fixed** (ce398ba) — the grade aside was gated on the viewport `xl:` breakpoint (1280) although the shell sidebar + submission list take ~620px; `main` is now a container (`@container/review`) and the two-column grid switches on `@[44rem]` of the content width, so 1280 stacks the aside, 1440 (and a collapsed sidebar at 1280) keep two columns. Verified with a standalone Playwright script at 1280×900 / 1440×900 (no bounding-box overlap, «Открыть»/«Предпросмотр» pass a trial click); no vitest for a class change. |
| UX-049 | F25 | With the «Оценка обновлена коллегой» notice open (unsaved local edits), «Опубликовать оценку» publishes the local draft over the colleague's version with no 412 (the refetched version is echoed) and the notice stays after publishing. | **fixed** (004fd5f) — `GradeForm` sent `If-Match` from the refetched `submission.version`, so a publish over the colleague's save was echoed back as 200, and the notice was only cleared by a reseed. Now save/publish/return (and Ctrl+Enter) are disabled with the hint «Сначала выберите, какую версию оставить.» (`Grading.Panel.staleDraftBlocked`, ru/kk/en) while the notice is open; `baseVersion` state carries the version the drafts were based on, «Загрузить их версию» reseeds, «Оставить мой черновик» keeps the drafts and adopts the new version explicitly, a successful save clears the notice (and `version` joins the reseed trigger). vitest review-controls «blocks save and publish behind the colleague notice…» |
| UX-050 | F47 (seam) | Learner applies → teacher's open collaboration page never shows the pending row on focus/visibility (queryClient `refetchOnWindowFocus:false, staleTime:60s`); teacher approves → learner's course page stays «…на рассмотрении / Отозвать заявку» after focus and client-side nav; the stale «Отозвать» then toasts a 403 «У вас нет прав…». | **fixed** — the contributors roster and `learner-state` queries set `staleTime: 5_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: true, refetchOnMount: true` (per-query; the client defaults stay); a stale «Отозвать» hitting 403/404 refetches and toasts «Заявка уже рассмотрена — статус обновлён» (ru/kk/en) instead of «нет прав»; server `remove_contributor`: self-delete with no row → 404 `not-found` (was the roster-manager 403). nextest contributors_flow applicant_withdraws_pending_application (second withdraw → 404); vitest landing-enrolled-cta «refetches and explains when a stale withdraw hits 403» |
| UX-051 | F47/F09 | «Вы являетесь автором» is plain text (no link to the workspace); two permanent `role=status/alert` live regions on the collaboration info panels; BUG-013 validation is toast-only (no field highlight). | **fixed** — «Вы являетесь автором» is an `AppLink` to `/dash/courses/{id}` (locale-prefixed); `CourseEditorNotice` renders `Alert` with `role="note"` (no permanent live region); collections/new sets `aria-invalid` + an inline `role=alert` message under the empty name/description and focuses the field (no toast). vitest new-collection-forbidden «marks the empty name invalid inline and focuses it» |

| UX-077 | F32/F31 | nits: analytics not-found pages return HTTP 200; kz sidebar «Оқытушы аналитикасы» vs page «Мұғалім аналитикасы». | **kk fixed (74e1bbf), 200 is the streaming contract** — `notFound()` already runs in the async server page (before any data await for the syntactic cases), but with `cacheComponents` the root layout's `<Suspense>` streams the `<html>` shell before any `[locale]` code runs, so every in-app `notFound()` (the invalid-locale layout guard and `/course/{bad}` included) commits `200` + the not-found UI; only unmatched routes (`/ru/no-such-route`) are a real 404. Next docs (streaming guide, «Status codes»): a status after the first byte is impossible; the alternatives are a `proxy.ts` rewrite for the syntactic cases or dropping the root Suspense (no streaming app-wide) — not worth it for a nit; left as is. kk: the sidebar tooltip is «Аналитика» like ru, its aria label «Мұғалім аналитикасын ашу» («Мұғалім» is the catalog's word for teacher, 29 uses); no «Оқытушы аналитикас…» remains. vitest kk-teacher-analytics-term |
