| UX-083 | F01 | Login: empty submit, then fill both fields and submit → stale «Обязательно» under both filled fields during the ~1.5 s pending window; the 429 banner gives no retry time although `loginAction` returns `retryAfterSeconds`. | `useActionState` keeps the previous result (its `fieldErrors`) until the action settles; `rate_limited` ignored `retryAfterSeconds`. Contract change: `UserProfile` (`GET/PATCH users/me`) gains `has_password` and `google_linked`, stored on the session record at login (password login: `true` + `google_accounts` lookup; Google login: `AUTHENTICATION_METHOD_TYPE_PASSWORD` from the auth-method listing it already made, `google_linked: true`; old records default to has-password). No set-password capability exists server-side, so a Google-only account sees «no password — keep signing in with Google» instead of the change form; the TOTP copy now says it guards password sign-in, plus a note that Google sign-in never asks for the code when Google is linked (ru/kk/en). Needs `just openapi` + `generate:api-types` (until then the page reads the flags optionally and defaults to the old layout). nextest `google_only_profile_has_no_password_and_a_google_link` (google_flow); vitest `user-security-settings` (Google-only case). |

Fix: (a) a preview skips the cap (and, like quiz previews after BUG-278, the late penalty); (b) InlineStatusStrip clamps used to the cap (server `attempts_remaining` already clamps at 0); (c) `dashboard.user_rank` is `rank_of(profile)` — `null` when opted out (schema: `user_rank` nullable; the web type was already `number \| null`). Checks: nextest file_submissions_flow `author_attempt_grade_and_download` (two author previews past `max_attempts 1`), gamification_flow `xp_flows_from_completion_and_admin_awards` (opted-out dashboard rank null), vitest inline-status-strip (2 used of 1 reads «1 из 1»). |

| UX-209 | F47/F10 | The server's 30 s request timeout is shorter than `MEMBER_WAIT` (60 s): a roster add while a member lock is busy answers 408 at 30 s although the row landed, the override was swept and the `progress:staff-change` job succeeded — a retry then gets 409. Post-commit re-projection must never hold the response past the timeout: wait inline only briefly (well under 30 s), then hand the rest to the job (BUG-305 mechanism) and answer the committed write's result. | **fixed** (this commit, with BUG-310) — root cause: `after_staff_change` ran the sweep inline with the 60 s member wait, longer than the 30 s request timeout, so a busy lock held the response until the TimeoutLayer answered 408 for a landed write. Fix: the shared `after_commit` path waits at most 5 s inline, then answers the committed write and leaves the rest to the still-running task plus a queued `progress:staff-change` job. nextest `roster_add_past_the_inline_wait_is_201_and_the_job_sweeps` (lock held past the wait → 201 in < 15 s, override kept under the lock, the queued job sweeps it after release). |
| UX-210 | F44/F38 | Found by the UX-209 builder (f0871d4): RBAC `propagate_all` (role delete / grant-set change) calls `after_staff_change` per member, each waiting up to 5 s inline — with ≥ 6 locked members a role change's response nears the 30 s timeout (a 408 for a write that landed). One inline budget for the whole propagation (run the members' re-projections concurrently or hand the batch to the job after a single bound); nextest with several locked members. | **fixed** — root cause: `propagate_all` awaited `propagate` (grant push + `after_staff_change`) per member in sequence, so each busy member spent its own 5 s `INLINE_WAIT`. Now the grant pushes stay sequential and the re-projections run concurrently via `join_all` of `after_staff_change`: one shared ≤ 5 s bound, the still-busy members left to their already-queued `progress:staff-change` jobs (members already pushed are still re-projected if a later push fails). nextest `assessments_access_flow::role_change_over_busy_members_waits_once` (4 locked members: 7 s vs 20 s; every job sweeps after release). (commit: see git log UX-210) |
| UX-211 | F01/F42/F04 | Identity hardening (pass 27 critic, code-read): password login stamps the session's `rbac_version` from the user row read **before** the epoch while grants are read after it (`auth.rs:508`; Google login reads both after) — a session can carry a version older than its grants, so the UX-205 CAS could accept an older propagate; read the version with the grants. `PATCH /users/me` avatar: a hang-up between `add_reference` and `replace_avatar` (`users.rs:92-95`) leaks the upload reference — one tx and/or detached. `DELETE /auth/sessions/{h}` and verify-email lose only their audit row on a hang-up — detach. Whitespace-only verify code → answer 422 without calling Zitadel. | **fixed** (this commit) — root causes: `login_attempt` took `rbac_version` from the pre-epoch lookup row while grants were read after the epoch; `claim_avatar` counted the reference on the pool before `replace_avatar` opened its tx; `revoke_session` / `verify_email` ran on the request future; `verify_email` sent the trimmed code to Zitadel unchecked. Fix: the post-epoch status re-read is `find_auth_user` and supplies `rbac_version` too (as Google login); the avatar claim goes through the shared `files::uploads::claim_upload` inside the swap+release tx (one `replace_avatar`); both routes run `detached()`; a blank code is the uniform 422 `code` before any lookup. nextest users_flow `dropped_avatar_patch_leaks_no_reference` (drops the PATCH as soon as the upload counts as referenced ×5 — no pinned non-avatar upload; fails on the old split), auth_flow `registration_creates_the_account_and_emails_the_code` (blank code → 422, no Zitadel call). Sibling not touched: `PlatformService::claim_branding` has the same claim-then-swap split (admin-only). |
| UX-212 | F13 | A learner whose session is revoked mid-attempt (session cap eviction / logout elsewhere) and who then changes an answer is silently redirected to `/ru/login?returnTo=…` with no notice; the changed answer is lost (the draft still holds the old one) — the learner-side twin of UX-031 (b21's draft PATCH 401 at 15:46:42Z). Keep the unsaved answer locally and tell the learner the session ended (toast / banner), restore it after re-login. | fixed — root cause: the attempt hook treated a save/submit 401 as a generic failure while the api-client redirected to login, so the unsaved answers died with the page. Now a 401 keeps them in sessionStorage keyed by the submission id and toasts `sessionEnded`; the redirect keeps returnTo; when that draft reopens the hook restores them once, toasts `sessionEndedRestored` and saves (ru/kk/en). vitest `session-ended-keeps-answers`. Commit: see `fix(web): UX-212` |
| UX-213 | F24 | Saving a restricted access list that drops a learner with graded work (L1, published 80) asks nothing and toasts «Доступ сохранен.»; L1's `submissions/me` / `submissions/{id}` then answer 403 — the learner can no longer read their own graded result. Confirm when the change removes learners with attempts (say they lose access), and keep a learner's own submissions/results readable after losing access to take the assessment. | **fixed** (8f53428 + orchestrator, this commit; reverify-E: result read-only + notice fixed) — with nothing handed in, the access-closed page showed the «Готовы начать» entry card with no way to start; it now shows only the notice. **fixed** (8f53428, 623574c, pass 27 builder-318b): `useAssessment` turned any attempt-state 403 into the page error. When the take gate refuses (403) but `submissions/me` answers, the hook builds a read-only attempt view (`accessClosed`, can_start/continue false, effective rules from the assessment policy): the published result / attempts render under a «Доступ к новым попыткам закрыт» notice (ru/kk/en); no course access still fails `submissions/me` and stays an error. vitest `attempt-access-closed`. Reopened (reverify-B pass 27): quiz page error. Earlier: **reopened** (reverify-B pass 27): the API reads are fixed, but the quiz page still shows only «Не удалось запустить эту активность… нет прав»: `useAssessment.ts:219` turns the attempt-state 403 into a page error, so the learner never sees their published result — while the new confirm dialog promises «Свои результаты они по-прежнему увидят». Earlier: **fixed** (990d5e5): own-attempt reads (`my_submissions`, `my_submission`) went through `attempt_state` → `require_submit_access`, whose allowlist gate is a take gate. They now use `reading_state` (course access + grant, no allowlist); attempt-state/start/save/submit keep it. The access tab counts learners with an attempt on this assessment (gradebook cells) who were in the saved audience and are not in the new one (group members fetched for listed groups) and confirms «N учащихся… больше не смогут проходить… свои результаты увидят» (ru/kk/en); nextest `progress_flow::a_learner_off_the_allowlist_still_reads_their_attempts`, vitest `asks before a save drops learners who already have attempts` |

A co-author removed from the roster while editing: autosave 403 shows only «Ошибка: Не удалось сохранить активность.» and the readiness server action returns 500 ×4 (`GET readiness` 403 unhandled) — say they lost access. kz: «Нобай» means both «draft» (filter/status) and «thumbnail» («Курс нобайы») — use a distinct word for the thumbnail (e.g. «мұқаба»). | **fixed** (c4277a8): the rename input is focused (ref, selected) and labelled «Название активности», and Esc/save hand focus back to the pencil; a 403 autosave is its own `forbidden` save state that stops autosave and says «У вас больше нет доступа к этому курсу — изменения не сохраняются» (indicator + explicit-save toast); readiness moved out of the `'use server'` courses service into `services/courses/readiness.ts` (client `apiJson`), so its 403 keeps its status (no 500, no retries — BUG-035); kk thumbnail strings say «мұқаба» (25 keys), «нобай» stays «draft»; vitest in activity-element-grants, activity-autosave-scope, editor-save-conflict |
